Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the reinforcement structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 215. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 203 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the reinforcement space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 215 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 203. QED.
